  same pass: reuse the existing `compareRole` comparator, and a single `LIVE_CONDUITS` source.

**Honest limit — the east-west conduit lanes do not render in the live co-served app.** The lanes
are **computed and golden-proven** in `layout/lanes.ts` (22 on the real bundle), but the S1
`CastSummary` DTO publishes only a conduit *count*, not the `{from, to}` pairs, so no API response
the SPA reaches carries the roster. S3 degrades honestly — an empty band with an in-UI "conduit list
absent from this API response" note — and the band **lights up with no view change** the moment a
future cast endpoint carries the conduits (`buildGraph` reads them where present). Recorded as
`docs/PRODUCT_GAPS.md` **GAP-079**; the fix is a small additive `CastSummaryDTO.conduits` backend
slice, outside this frontend-only task.

**Acceptance — the gate (no live-verify; frontend + tests only, nothing deploys or touches a
cluster):** `npm run typecheck` exit 0 (strict `tsc --noEmit`, clean **from zero** — LES-042's
tolerated ~118-error baseline belongs to the Grafana plugin and is not inherited); `npm run test`
**187 passed / 6 skipped** (the six are the opt-in `AXRANGE_LIVE_API` drift test); `npm run build`
exit 0; goldens deterministic (regenerated twice, byte-identical). The full pytest suite is
unaffected (2693 passed / 12 skipped, 2705 collected) and `doc-stats.py --check` is clean.

**Remaining sequence:** **S4 (pins)** is next, then S5 (editing + save), S6 (apply + refresh — its
aximprint gate stays lifted, S1/§12.8), S7 (the Grafana embed, deliberately last).

**Affected docs:**
- `docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` — status header → "S3 BUILT"; new §14 "S3 built as"; §5.2
  locality wording corrected; sequence re-ordered so S4 is next.
- `o2/console/README.md` — status → S3, the drawn levels, the `layout:golden` workflow, the
  conduit-lane note.
- `docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` — §12 built-status touch (which O-defaults S3 consumed).
- `docs/LESSONS.md` — LES-260 (jsdom `import.meta.url`), LES-261 (ordered-column locality),
  LES-262 (precedence vs alphabetical ordering).
- `docs/PRODUCT_GAPS.md` — GAP-079 (conduit lanes absent from the live `CastSummary`).
- `docs/SESSION_STATE.md` — Console thread → S3 BUILT; next steps → S4.

## 2026-09-08 — Console S2 BUILT: the SPA scaffold (`o2/console/`), and §12.6 settled as a suppressed-key set delta

**Authority:** **implementation (Claude Code)** — for the build, for the §12.6 ruling below, and for
the kept deviations. The design this implements is **Authority: user**:
`docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` (ratified 2026-09-04 — a clean-start SPA, the URL as the zoom,
three panes, the six-class evidence encoding) with `docs/design/14-operator-assertions.md` §2–§4.
`docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` S2's slicing, file layout and mechanical spellings were already
recorded there as implementation calls open to veto; this entry records what the build **changed
about them**, which is the part §3 cannot tell a later reader.

**Version-locked: nothing moved.** No `IR_SCHEMA`/`PLANNER`/`OP_CATALOG`/`VALIDATOR`/`ADAPTER`/
`IMAGE_MANIFEST` change, no new op type (**40**), no rule (**114**), no `scenario_schema_version`
or state-store `SCHEMA_VERSION` bump. One new component directory under `o2/`, one additive DTO
field, and a history fallback on an already-existing mount.

**What changed:**

- **`o2/console/` — a new component directory** (Vite 8 + React 18 + TypeScript strict; exact pins
  `react`/`react-dom` 18.3.1, `react-router` 7.18.3, `vite` 8.2.2, `typescript` 5.9.2, `vitest`
  5.0.0, `jsdom` 30.0.1, `@testing-library/react` 16.3.3, `engines: node >=22.12`). The three-pane
  shell (outline / map / inspector), the router, a typed client, hooks, the edit store, the theme,
  the build and the co-serve — **and nothing drawn**: the map pane renders the cast's live summary,
  which is the point of the slice. `o2/console/` never imports from the Grafana plugin and the
  plugin never imports from it (`15` §9: the SPA starts clean).
- **The URL grammar is defined once, in `src/model/zoom.ts`, and is pure** — `/imprints/<id>`,
  `…/segments/<cidr>` (**the prefix length written with a hyphen**, `10.80.20.0-24`, never `%2F`),
  `…/hosts/<key>` in both the flat and the segment-nested spelling, `…/matrix` (with `?view=matrix`
  accepted as an alias and normalized to the path), behind a **single catch-all route** so
  `routes.tsx` holds no second copy of the grammar. `?ghosts=`, `?class=`, `?occupancy_lt=` parse
  into typed state and **every unusable value is reported in `problems`, never silently dropped**;
  a malformed `%` escape is an error, not a fall-back to the catalog root.
- **A typed API client over the eight S1 routes plus the listing** (`api/client.ts`, `context.ts`,
  `hooks.ts`, `types.ts`): one fetch path, abort on unmount, and typed error kinds
  (400/401/404/409/412/422/502/503/network/malformed) carrying the server's `detail` and the etag a
  failed apply returns. The **write token is held in a module variable for the life of the tab** —
  never compiled in, never in `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`/a cookie — proven by a test grepping
  the built bundle.
- **The model and edit layers, React- and API-free:** `model/keys.ts` (entity keys
  `hostname:`/`address:`/`mac:`, CIDR ↔ route encoding, site keys in the hyphen form the server's
  own regex accepts), `model/evidence.ts` (the six classes in precedence order, `compiler` never
  authorable, **absent confidence is not zero**, `countByClass` reporting unknown classes rather
  than dropping them), `edit/store.ts` (pending / saved / applied per `15` §8; **a 412 leaves the
  pending edit intact, REPORTS the server's etag in the conflict record and never adopts it**, and
  sets `mustReload` so a save cannot be re-issued until a real re-read; `applied` matched by block
  and key; a document belonging to a different imprint is refused; `problems[]` surfaced).
- **`theme/tokens.css`** — light/dark via `prefers-color-scheme`, the six-class evidence palette
  (each class carrying a fill style as well as a hue, so `observed` and `referenced` stay
  distinguishable in monochrome) plus the pending/saved states. `15` §12 leaves the hues open for a
  `dataviz` pass before S3's first screen; this is that pass's starting point, not its verdict.
- **Python, two permitted changes.** (1) `axrange/api/app.py` gained the **SPA history fallback**
  (`_SpaFiles` / `_spa_wants_index` / `_spa_cache_control`): a deep client route gets `index.html`,
  while `/api/*` (tested on the **decoded** path, so `%61pi` is excluded too) and asset-suffix
  misses stay JSON/404s, write methods are 405, the index is `Cache-Control: no-cache` and
  `/assets/` is `public, max-age=31536000, immutable`. **Headless is byte-identical when
  `--spa-dir` is unset.** (2) `ImprintDiffDTO.suppressed_keys` settles §12.6 (below).
- **Dev and co-serve.** `npm run dev` binds `127.0.0.1:5173` explicitly (Vite 8 defaults to
  `localhost`, which resolves to `[::1]` here) and proxies `/api` to `AXRANGE_DEV_API` (default
  `http://127.0.0.1:8080`); `VITE_AXRANGE_API` is a **different** variable meaning only "compile in
  an absolute base URL, bypass the proxy, and require CORS the API does not implement". Co-serve:
  `axrange serve --spa-dir o2/console/dist --imprints-dir twin-configs-cast`.
- **Tests.** Vitest **119 passed / 6 skipped** (the six are a live-API drift test that runs when
  `AXRANGE_LIVE_API` is set — it ran green against both real bundles). pytest **+75** in
  `tests/test_api_console.py` (367 there now), including a **DTO field-set guard for the nine
  Console-read DTOs whose failure message names `o2/console/src/api/types.ts`** and seven traversal
  encodings proving no file outside `--spa-dir` is ever served. Full suite **2693 passed / 12
  skipped (2705 collected)**. `npm run typecheck` is clean **from zero** — LES-042's tolerated
  ~118-error baseline belongs to the Grafana plugin and is not inherited.

**The §12.6 ruling — a suppressed-key SET DELTA, and a per-removal column refused.** S1 left the
canvas unable to join a twin-side host removal back to the authored key that caused it
(`GET …/diff`'s twin side is keyed by the twin's own node ids, so a `suppress` keyed on
`address:10.1.10.222` appears there as `generic-endpoint-10-1-1-core` disappearing). The
orchestrator recommended a per-removal `authored_key` column; **the build refused it as unfaithful**
— the sidecar's `population.suppressed` rows are `{key, note, placed, matched}` and **never name the
twin node a suppression removed**, and the twin's group ids are minted by aximprint's own
role × segment × rung rule, which axRange must not reimplement. With two suppressions and a gate
removal inside one interval, such a column would attribute by correlation and present the result as
a join. Published instead: `ImprintDiffDTO.suppressed_keys` (a set delta computed by
`recompile.suppressed_key_delta` from the accounting **both** snapshots already hold, via one shared
`_suppressed_keys` normalizer; `null` — never empty — with a warning stating what was measured when
either side lacks it; a suppression counts as `moved`), plus the durable authority that needs no
fingerprint at all: `population.suppressed[]` on `GET …/provenance`, where `matched: false` separates
an ineffective record from a real suppression. **The capability knowingly given up:** clicking a
suppression record and being shown the specific host it removed, when two or more suppressions land
in one apply. **Residual for S6:** nothing in the suite proves the *real* compiler writes the key
into the **new** sidecar on an apply — the tests doctor the sidecar at the correct seam and the
producer source confirms it, but the first live apply through the SPA is that proof.

**Three rounds, and what each round's green suite had certified.** The scaffold; then the review's
three must-fix — a **412 that adopted the server's etag**, so a retry would have silently
overwritten the other writer (last-writer-**refused** inverted into last-writer-**wins**, with the
guard still passing); the dev override `VITE_AXRANGE_API` being lifted into the bundle by Vite's
`loadEnv`, so the browser bypassed the very proxy it was configuring and went cross-origin to an API
with no CORS; and a **failed** provenance read rendering as "no sidecar on this bundle" — plus ten
should-fix; then the tester's four — a query value parsed with `Number()`, so `occupancy_lt=+`
became a silent zero filter; duplicate query keys and a `?view=` at the wrong level silently
ignored; and the store not checking `imprint_id` on load. All closed with revert-proven tests; no
residual defect.

**Deviations from the work order's S2 text, all KEPT** (`docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` §13.3
supersedes those lines): `model/zoom.ts` and `api/context.ts` added to §4's layout (the URL grammar
must be pure and round-trip-testable while `routes.tsx` is React; the client is *provided* rather
than imported, which is what lets a test render the real shell against a fake client); one catch-all
route; both host-route spellings; the `?view=matrix` alias; three dev dependencies beyond the named
set (`@testing-library/dom`, `jsdom`, `@types/node`); and the SPA history fallback landing in
`axrange/api/` inside an SPA slice. **Documented behaviors, not defects:** a deep route is HTML
regardless of `Accept` (the rule that protects a caller is the `/api/` exclusion, and
`Accept`-branching would make one URL answer differently to `curl` and to a browser); a missing
`.html` path falls back to the app; sourcemaps are served.

**Why:**

- S3 draws the canvas; it needed a frame that is **correct** rather than finished-looking — a
  router, a client, an edit store and a theme that are each independently testable. The map pane
  showing live cast numbers is the acceptance criterion in disguise: if those are right against a
  real bundle, the client, the hooks and the co-serve are right.
- §12.6 was S2's first item by the previous handoff's own ordering, and it is a **contract**
  question, not a UI one — deciding it with the SPA's needs visible is exactly why S1 deferred it.
- The history fallback is not optional for this design: `15` §7 makes **the URL the zoom**, so a
  bookmark, a reload or a pasted link lands on a path that exists only in the SPA's router. Without
  it every deep Console route 404s on reload while working perfectly when navigated to in-app.

**Affected docs:**

- `docs/CHANGELOG.md` — this entry.
- `docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` — status header (**S1 + S2 BUILT; S3–S7 OPEN**) and a new
  §13 "S2 built as", carrying the §12.6 ruling, the kept deviations, the rounds and the S6 residual.
- `docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` — §7's URL grammar and §8's etag-refusal rule restated in the
  present tense as built (the hyphen CIDR, `occupancy_lt`, the `?view=matrix` alias; *report the
  etag, never adopt it, reload before retry*), and §9's build/serve facts.
- `CLAUDE.md` — the golden-twin blockquote gains S2; the pytest counter is reconciled 2630 → 2705.
- `docs/LESSONS.md` — LES-251..LES-259.
- `docs/SESSION_STATE.md` — the handoff narrative.
- `o2/console/README.md` — new, written by the build: quick start, the URL table, the two
  non-interchangeable environment variables, the evidence palette, the §12.6 decision, and the four
  documented co-serve behaviors.
- **Riding in the same commit, from the main session and not part of this build:** a **sixth
  documentation genre** — the **reference note** (`docs/CONVENTIONS.md` §3) — and its first
  instance, `docs/reference/twin-from-topology-diagram.md` (+ `docs/reference/README.md`): an
  explanation recorded from a design discussion, weighing an option against the platform as it
  stands and naming what would have to be built. Neither a work order nor a design-of-record; when
  one turns into work it gets a work order and the note stays as the reasoning trail.

**Affected code:**

- `o2/console/` — new (31 checked-in files: `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `vite.config.ts`,
  `tsconfig.json`, `index.html`, `README.md`, `.gitignore`, `src/` — `main.tsx`, `App.tsx`,
  `routes.tsx`, `api/{client,context,hooks,types}.ts`, `model/{keys,evidence,zoom}.ts`,
  `edit/store.ts`, `theme/{tokens,shell}.css`, `views/{Outline,MapPane,Inspector}.tsx` — and
  `test/`).
- `axrange/api/app.py` — `_SPA_ASSET_SUFFIXES`, `_spa_wants_index`, `_spa_cache_control`,
  `_SpaFiles`; `_maybe_mount_spa` mounts it.
- `axrange/api/dtos.py`, `axrange/api/service.py`, `axrange/api/recompile.py` —
  `ImprintDiffDTO.suppressed_keys`, `recompile.suppressed_key_delta`, `_suppressed_keys`.
- `tests/test_api_console.py` — +75 (367 total).

**Links:** `docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` §13 · `docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` §7–§9 ·
`o2/console/README.md` · CHANGELOG 2026-09-06 "Console S1 BUILT" (§12.6's origin).

## 2026-09-06 — Console S1 BUILT: the assertions / apply / diff API slice (`axrange/api/`)

**Authority:** **implementation (Claude Code)** — for the build and for all four rulings below
(R1–R4). The design this implements is **Authority: user**:
`docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` §10 (the endpoint table, ratified 2026-09-04) and
`docs/design/14-operator-assertions.md` §2–§4 (the six-class evidence model, the assertions file as
the contract, save vs apply). The slicing, endpoint mechanics and the five calls of
`docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` §8.1 were already recorded there as implementation calls open
to veto; this entry records what the build **changed about them**, which is the part a reader
cannot infer from the design.

**Version-locked: nothing moved.** No `IR_SCHEMA`/`PLANNER`/`OP_CATALOG`/`VALIDATOR`/`ADAPTER`/
`IMAGE_MANIFEST` change, no new op type (**40**), no rule (**114**), no `scenario_schema_version`
or state-store `SCHEMA_VERSION` bump. Additive routes, new DTOs, two new leaf modules and one
vendored schema file.

**What changed:**

- **Two new leaf modules under `axrange/api/`.**
  - **`console_files.py`** — the two operator files that live beside a cast bundle:
    `<id>.assertions.yaml` (`axr-assertions/1`) and `layout.json` (`axr-layout/1`). Reads and
    writes both with a **content-hash etag**, a **per-file blocking lock**, and an atomic
    `mkstemp` + `os.replace` write that **preserves the target's mode** (0644 for a new file).
    Assertion validation **derives its closed sets at import time from the vendored schema** —
    record kinds, per-kind `override.field` sets, the `add` field sets — rather than restating
    them. Author-facing behaviour worth knowing: `as_of` is coerced from the `datetime` PyYAML
    resolves a bare ISO timestamp to; `kind` is stripped on write except on `add` records (where
    the compiler requires it) and derived on read; a non-IP `address:` key is **warned, not
    refused**, while a strict CIDR in an `address:` key **is** refused, naming the network form;
    key order is preserved on dump.
  - **`recompile.py`** — a `RecompileBackend` protocol plus `SubprocessRecompile`, which runs
    **both producer stages**: `aximprint cast --store <STORE> --pit <pit_id> --out <scratch>
    --imprint-id <id> [--relocate X] [--max-count N] [--no-chatter] --assertions <file>`, then
    `aximprint install-cast <scratch> <catalog-root> <id> [--sensors NAME ...]`. The cast's flags
    are **read from the bundle's own `cast_invocation` block** (`bundle_schema:
    axr-topology/1.5`) and a bundle without one is a **422 naming the block**; the sensors are
    recovered from the bundle's `tap-cast.yaml`. Change detection is a **content fingerprint**
    axRange computes (sha256 over `<id>.yaml`, `<id>.topology.json`, `<id>.provenance.json`),
    which is also the key of `.history/<fp>.json` (keep 5, versioned `axr-bundle-snapshot/1`; a
    snapshot carries **both** the observed population and the twin's emitted population, plus the
    sidecar's `population` block). Typed failures: producer exit 2 → **422**; any other non-zero
    or a timeout → **502** (naming a partially-installed bundle when stage 2 fails after stage 1);
    an unset or unusable `AXRANGE_AXIMPRINT_BIN` / `AXRANGE_AXIMPRINT_STORE` → **503 naming the
    key**. `DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_S` is 120 s, with a proxy note.
- **`axrange/api/schemas/axr-assertions-1.schema.json`** — a **byte-identical vendored copy** of
  aximprint's published `src/aximprint/schemas/axr-assertions-1.schema.json`. Two drift tests arm
  it: a **hard** one comparing the closed sets and a **soft** one comparing bytes, both resolving
  the sibling repo through `AXIMPRINT_REPO` or `../aximprint-axrange` and skipping when it is
  absent. `pyproject.toml` gained package-data for the schema only.
- **Eight new routes** (`app.py`, `service.py`, `imprints.py`, `dtos.py`), all under
  `/api/imprints/{id}`: `GET …/topology` (a thin **alias** delegating to the shipped
  `topology_export`, pinned by an identity test), `GET …/cast`, `GET`/`PUT …/assertions`,
  `GET`/`PUT …/layout`, `POST …/apply`, `GET …/diff?since=`. Writes are write-token gated. The
  etag may arrive in `If-Match` **or** in the body — disagreeing values are refused — and a stale
  or missing etag is **412**. `POST …/apply` is **synchronous** (a sync `def` FastAPI runs off the
  event loop) behind a **per-imprint lock**: a concurrent apply is **409**, and a `PUT` while an
  apply runs is refused 409; the etag is accepted from header, body or the document. `GET
  …/diff?since=<unknown-fingerprint>` is **404 naming the known set**; the `observed` and `twin`
  sides are reported separately with a `twin_config_changed` flag, and an absent section is
  **null with a warning**, never an empty list.
- **Listing became tolerant** — it now also admits canonical-sidecar bundles and publishes
  `skipped: [{id, reason}]`. `mean_confidence` is computed from the canonical sidecar over the
  fields that carry one, with the coverage published in `confidence_source` (e.g. *"canonical
  sidecar (151/687 fields)"*); `has_confidence`'s docstrings were corrected and a warning counts
  confidence-less rows by class. **One positive id regex** — `[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*` — is
  shared by `resolve_imprint_dir` (the **one** traversal guard) and the service.
- **The class vocabulary is asymmetric on purpose:** `compiler` is **never authorable** in an
  assertions file (five classes) but is still publishable in a provenance row (six classes).
- **Rider on `axrange/fidelity/reference.py`:** the sidecar-absent warning now describes the
  *bundle* ("installed before the installer copied the sidecar, or produced by an older cast"),
  since aximprint's installer **does** copy it now; read-only `bundle_schema`, `code_fingerprint`
  and `cast_invocation` fields were added. **No scoring effect** — `to_dict()["fingerprint"]` is
  untouched. (Scorecard run 1 was measured against this working tree; CHANGELOG 2026-09-05
  (later) records that.)
- **Plumbing:** `axrange serve --imprints-dir` (`cli/main.py`, `serve.py`, `settings.py`);
  `.gitignore` gains `**/.history/`; the `mini-cast` / `mini-cast-prov` fixtures gained a
  `cast_invocation` block under 1.5 and a `population` block.

**Four rulings, recorded because they bind future work:**

1. **aximprint's compiler is the reader of record for `axr-assertions/1`.** axRange's writer
   accepts and refuses exactly what the compiler does — no more, no less.
2. **The vocabulary is a vendored artifact the code derives from, never a restated literal.**
   Three axRange-only rules live in code with their reasons attached (`imprint` required;
   `segment` and `name` required on an `add` — the compiler fails both at compile time anyway).
3. **The apply runs BOTH producer stages and reads its argv from the bundle**, refusing by name
   when `cast_invocation` is absent. A cast alone does not update a bundle: it writes generic
   stems, and only `install-cast` names them `<id>.*`.
4. **The change detector is a content fingerprint, never the build hash.** `code_fingerprint` is
   the compiler's build hash, identical across two different casts of the same code — using it
   made `changed` permanently false and turned keep-5 into keep-1.

**Four deviations from `docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md`, KEPT — the work order is reconciled to
them (§12), not the code to it:**

- **412, not 409, on a stale etag** (409 is now the *apply-in-progress* answer, a different
  situation with a different recovery).
- **`diff?since=<unknown>` is 404**, naming the known fingerprints, rather than `{known: false}`.
- **Apply is synchronous behind a per-imprint lock**, not `apply_id` + poll. D2(a)'s "a background
  thread inside `axrange serve`" holds structurally; the lock is **process-local**, which is
  acceptable for one `axrange serve` and is stated as a limit. A reverse proxy needs
  `proxy_read_timeout` ≥ 120 s.
- **Tolerant listing admits canonical-sidecar bundles too.** D3(a) named only the sidecar-*less*
  case; without the widening, `corpapcn-golden` — the only fully provenanced bundle there is —
  was invisible to the Console. Overrides are published as `evidence_class`.

**Why:**

The Console's whole loop is *display → edit → save → recompile → refresh*, and every step after
"edit" is a cross-repo file contract. Building the API half first means the contract is exercised
against the **real compiler** long before an SPA exists to drive it — which is exactly what caught
the four defects unit tests could not (below).

**Three code rounds, and what each round's green suite had certified:**

- **Round 1** (81 tests) passed its unit tests and was **broken end to end** against the real
  compiler, found by live integration: the written file carried `kind` and the compiler refused it
  (`suppress[0]: unknown key(s) ['kind']`); the apply never updated the bundle (the cast writes
  generic stems; only the install stage names them); `code_fingerprint` is the build hash, so
  `changed` was always false and keep-5 was keep-1; and the real stdout has no `record_counts`.
  The reviewer found five must-fix in the same round: a `%2e`-encoded id reaching a `PUT` and
  writing into the catalog root; PID-named temp files colliding across threadpool requests (one
  500, one **silent overwrite**); a hand-authored `as_of` refused because YAML resolves it to a
  `datetime`; `value: null` on an override dropped by the serializer, so a GET→PUT round trip
  broke; and `OSError`s escaping as 500.
- **Round 2** fixed those plus fifteen should-fix items.
- **Round 3** fixed the tester's two new live findings — stdout **clipped before it was parsed**
  (the real cast prints 8356 chars against an 8000-char cap, tail-kept, producing a false "not
  JSON" warning), and a `suppress` **invisible to the diff** because only the observed side was
  snapshotted, with a warning that asserted the kind of change it could not know — and the
  reviewer's four: `mkstemp` narrowing an existing file to 0600; `sensors_from_tap_plan`
  collapsing five distinguishable degraded states into one silent empty tuple; unversioned
  snapshots read by a later release as "everything added"; and apply not accepting the etag inside
  the document.

**Final independent proof, on the real compiler** (not a fake backend): the pinned argv reproduces
`--relocate 10.200.0.0/16` from the bundle (10.200.x output, not source CIDRs); stage 2 lands the
`<id>.*` files with `XPress-SW-A` preserved; the fingerprint moves; `record_counts` reads
`{add: 0, override: 0, suppress: 1}` with `cast_counts` populated; the diff names the suppressed
host **on the twin side** (`generic-endpoint-10-1-1-core` for `address:10.1.10.222`, and a
different key removes a different id) with the observed side unchanged and no warning; a second
identical apply reports no change; mode 0644 is preserved; wall time is **2.7 s per HTTP apply**
(the cast alone 0.74 s for 786 nodes); and a bundle without `cast_invocation` is a 422 naming the
block.

**Residual, recorded as S2's first item — not a defect:** the diff's twin side is keyed by **twin
node ids** and does not publish the sidecar's `population.suppressed[]` (which the snapshot
already holds), so the canvas cannot join a removal back to the authored key **from the diff
alone**.

**Two corrections the writing surfaced:**

- `docs/design/14-operator-assertions.md` §3's example omitted `note` on the host `add` and on the
  `override`, while the compiler requires a non-empty `note` on **all three** record kinds — the
  example is fixed.
- The sidecar's `population.emitted_hosts` is a **count** (156) and differs from the twin's node
  instances (157) by the synthetic `gw` router. Two different quantities, one similar name.

**Tests:** `tests/test_api_console.py` — **292 tests**. Full suite **2618 passed / 12 skipped /
0 failed / 0 xfailed (2630 collected)**; `CLAUDE.md`'s pytest counter is reconciled here, as the
2026-09-05 (later) entry said it would be.

**The producer half** landed in aximprint as a separate commit on its own `wip/golden-twin`:
`cast_invocation` under `axr-topology/1.5`, the `install-cast` subcommand (the shipped script
becomes a thin wrapper), the published schema with a parser-agreement test, and a recast of the
golden candidate (only the topology export changed, by the two expected keys).

**Affected docs:**
- `docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` — new §12 "S1 built as"; the status header
- `docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` — §10, the endpoint table's mechanics in present tense
- `docs/design/14-operator-assertions.md` — §3's example and the five/six-class asymmetry
- `CLAUDE.md` — the golden-twin blockquote; the pytest counter (2330 → 2630)
- `docs/LESSONS.md` — LES-230..LES-243
- `docs/SESSION_STATE.md`

**Affected code:** `axrange/api/console_files.py` (new), `axrange/api/recompile.py` (new),
`axrange/api/schemas/axr-assertions-1.schema.json` (new, vendored),
`axrange/api/{app,dtos,imprints,serve,service,settings}.py`, `axrange/cli/main.py`,
`axrange/fidelity/reference.py` (rider), `pyproject.toml`, `.gitignore`,
`tests/test_api_console.py` (new), `tests/fixtures/cast-bundles/{mini-cast,mini-cast-prov}/`.

**Links:** `docs/WORK_ORDER_console_canvas.md` §8.1/§12; `docs/design/15-console-canvas.md` §10;
`docs/design/14-operator-assertions.md` §3.

## 2026-09-06 — Services §9.2 gated `dev45` live-verify PASSED: a real server answers, and the sensor names the protocol

**Authority:** **implementation (Claude Code)** for the measurement and for every call made during
the run; **user** for the go-ahead on each mutating step, and the **user personally ran the two
`o2 deploy` commands** — that verb is blocked for agents by the permission classifier. The gate
itself (`docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md` §9.2, mandatory) and the row-5 criterion it
proves (`docs/design/13-golden-twin-fidelity.md` §3.3) are **Authority: user**.

**Subject:** `twin-configs/svc-verify/` (the throwaway seven-VM twin, commit `fbe70f87`) — never
the standing `te corpapcn-twin-0e4f7d58`, which is do-not-destroy and would trip `--update`'s
mutation refusal if given a declared listen set. Everything ran in a **dedicated scratch state
DB**; `~/.axrange/state.db` and `/home/rocky/axr-product/deploy-dev45/state.db` were never opened
for write.

**Result: PASSED, all six steps.** The claim this gate exists to test — *the sensor records the
protocol, not bare `tcp`/`udp`* — held: the FAIL condition never occurred on any classifiable flow.

**What changed:** nothing in the product. This entry is a **measurement**, plus eleven gaps and
fourteen lessons it produced, plus a README rewrite it forced.

### The run

| step | result |
|---|---|
| **1. `te deploy`** | `svc-verify-92fd7a79` (plan `svc-verify-14da1c1a`, the pinned id) — **82/82 op rows completed** (72 action + 10 barriers) in **17.3 min**. The DC path is 8.6 min of it: `wait-domain-ready` 332 s, the ADDS install 182 s, the join 53 s. One Windows first-boot QGA `guest-exec` timeout was **absorbed by the retry**, not a failure. |
| **2. in-guest `ss`** | every declared listen pair present on a **non-loopback** address on all five server hosts; `systemd-resolved` displaced on both bind9 hosts (the recipes' own step); `win-member`'s `137/udp` present. **One delta:** `svc-web` also exposes **LLMNR `5355/tcp+udp` on the wildcard**, put there by the image's `systemd-resolved` — so §9.2's *"nothing beyond the declared set + tcp/22"* exclusivity clause **cannot hold on a stock Debian cloud image** (**GAP-075**). |
| **3. protocol probes** | **all six PASS** from `probe-client`. `dig @10.66.10.11 axdc04.corp.axr.lab` → NOERROR, `aa`, `A 10.66.10.11`. `curl --resolve` → **200 over a completed handshake**, CN/SAN `web-tier-10-80-72-core.corp.axr.lab`. `ntpdate -q` → an offset at **stratum 3** (chrony synced *through the router*, not the recipe's orphan stratum 10). `ldapsearch -s base` → rootDSE with `DC=corp,DC=axr,DC=lab`. `smbclient -L` → **anonymous session accepted with an empty share table** (Server 2022 denies anonymous enumeration); NETLOGON/SYSVOL listed when authenticated as `alice`. `kinit alice@CORP.AXR.LAB` → a TGT. `win-member` `PartOfDomain: True`. |
| **2b. `tap deploy`** | `tap-svc-verify-92fd7a79-55c62f75` — **7/7 ops in 20.7 s**; gretap `tapg_1295ea` to `10.255.252.10`; mirrors on the five `svc` ports with the `gw`'s other legs correctly **de-scoped**; **`verify-capture` TRUE** with a real sentinel (LES-001's rule, honored). |
| **4. at the sensor** | `event_type` histogram for the twin's subnets: **alert 49, dns 30, flow 24, smb 15, krb5 5, dcerpc 3**. **DNS PASS** (30 `dns` events + `flow` `app_proto dns`). **Kerberos PASS** (5 `krb5` events; `app_proto krb5` on TCP/88). **SMB PASS** (15 `smb` events; `app_proto smb` on TCP/445). **NTP PASS via the documented fallback** — no `ntp` event because the sensor render's `eve-log` types omit it (**GAP-077**); `flow` `app_proto ntp` on UDP/123. **TLS PASS on substance** — 9 flows `app_proto tls` on TCP/443, definitively **not** bare `tcp` — but **zero `tls` event records** although `tls` is enabled with `extended: yes` (**GAP-076**). **LDAP** came back `app_proto=failed` on 389/tcp+udp: unclassified by Suricata **7.0.10** (the `ldap` type is 8.0), reported and **not scored**, exactly as the README's version caveat prescribes. **DCERPC** classified on TCP/135 and on a dynamic port — a bonus nobody asked for. **The FAIL condition — bare `tcp`/`udp` where classifiable — did not occur.** |
| **5. `tfc deploy`** | `tfc-svc-verify-92fd7a79-a5adee95` in **6 s**; the persona ran as a transient `systemd-run` unit; **32 invocations across four cycles with `fails=0 missing_tool=0`**. Then a **278 s window with no operator probe at all**: 28 flows / 16 dns / 16 alerts from `probe-client`, `app_proto` **tls 10, ntp 10, dns 8**, plus ICMP — **all four persona emits arrive classified, from the persona's own cadence**. |
| **6. `te destroy`** | by explicit ids with `--allow-destroy`: tfc (persona stopped), tap (5 mirror de-scopes, gretap removed), te (**17 resources, 0 not cleared, 54 s**) → **152 VMs** (the 151 baseline + the dedicated sensor); the standing twin's **138 untouched**; gretaps back to **26**; no leftover tap/mirror/zone of the throwaway; `img` / `o2` / `XPress-SW*` / `Tcpdump-SW-A` untouched; the standing sensor byte-identical. |

**One disclosed guest write:** `/etc/krb5.conf` on `probe-client`, exactly as the README prescribes
— client-side probe setup, not a repair. It is nonetheless a finding: **no automated consumer can
obtain a ticket until realm configuration becomes twin content** (**GAP-074**).

### The sensor saga — the most consequential finding, recorded in full

Preflight found the standing `o2-suricata` sensor (VM 883610) **up, with Suricata DEAD since
2026-07-27 06:05** ("signal received"). It had been launched by `deploy-sensor` as a bare `-D`
daemon rather than a systemd unit: the 2026-07-17 supervision fix moved **tcpdump and zeek** to
`systemd-run` units and **never covered Suricata**. `o2 status` reported nothing wrong for **40
days**; only the Loki shipper survived. (**GAP-067**, with **LES-247**: for a sensor, "exists" is
not the check — "exists, running, and fresh" is.)

Worse, the standing surface `o2-surface-025c0709` lives in
`/home/rocky/axr-product/deploy-dev45/state.db`, **not** the twin's DB, so the sensor id does not
resolve from the twin's DB and `tap deploy` fails at resolve with `unknown sensor 'o2-suricata' --
not in the standing O2 inventory (axrange o2 status)` — **an error that points at a command which,
run against the other DB, confirms the sensor exists** (**GAP-069**). The surface manifest is
checked in **nowhere** (**GAP-068**); it was reconstructed from op inputs and the plan id
brute-forced to `o2-surface-acd8d24a-o2`.

**Option chosen first (user): re-assert the surface in place** into the scratch DB. The user ran
it; it **FAILED at `clone-vm`** with `template_vmid missing — check image binding` — the
`deploy-dev45` images root has no `suricata-sensor` binding, while the `axr-product` root the
standing surface was deployed from does. The agent had specified `--images` without checking that
*this sensor's* binding existed under it: LES-139's shape, one layer up (**LES-248**). By then
**five SDN ops had already run and created a second vnet `o2vb904f`** in the shared zone
`o2zbe2d1` with the same CIDR — because commit `7d33854e` (2026-07-27) made vnet names
**plan-unique one day AFTER** the standing surface was deployed, so current code cannot compute
the standing vnet's name and **adopted nothing** (**GAP-070**). A re-run with the right images root
would have adopted the stray vnet and **moved the standing sensor's NIC onto an address-less
bridge**. The stray vnet is inert (no IP, no subnet, no VM) and awaits a manual cleanup; the failed
deploy row `o2-surface-5274be37` is **deliberately not destroyed**, because its rows include the
**adopted shared zone**. Two more gaps fell out of the same failure: `clone-vm` validates
`template_vmid` **before** its adoption check (`rest.py` ~668–679), so adopting an existing VM
needs a binding it never uses (**GAP-071**); and a missing image binding is **silent at plan time**
(`template_vmid: null` persisted) and only fails on the cluster, after the SDN ops ran
(**GAP-072**). `tap validate` returned **PASS** against the `failed` surface deploy whose sensor
has no vmid (**GAP-073**).

**Option chosen second (user): a dedicated sensor.** The user ran `o2 deploy` of a scratch manifest
(`surface: o2-svcverify`, transport `10.255.252.0/24`, one `suricata-sensor` `svc-suricata`, no
export) with `--images /home/rocky/axr-product/deploy-dev45` → **`o2-svcverify-90f643c9`, 15/15 ops
in 1.21 min**, VM **665273** `o2-svc-suricata` at `10.255.252.10`, **Suricata 7.0.10 `Engine
started`**, `eve.json` growing. The standing sensor's `eve.json` remained frozen at 2026-07-27 **to
the nanosecond**.

**Two operator commands are still pending on `dev45`** (both blocked for agents by the classifier
or requiring `pvesh`):

```sh
axrange o2 destroy o2-svcverify-90f643c9 --allow-destroy --adapter-config adapter.conf --db <scratch>
#   blast radius verified from its 15 op rows: zone o2zda56b, vnet o2vd958f,
#   subnet 10.255.252.0/24, VM 665273 -- no row names any standing object.

pvesh delete /cluster/sdn/vnets/o2vb904f && pvesh set /cluster/sdn
#   the inert stray vnet from the failed re-assert.
```

### Deltas from the README, all recorded rather than smoothed

Wall time **17.3 min** against a 30–45 min budget; **stratum 3**, not the recipe's orphan 10;
anonymous `smbclient -L` lists **no** shares (Server 2022 posture — the expected output was written
against the wrong security posture, **LES-244**); `svc-web`'s **5355**; and — the one that matters
for anyone re-running this — **the README's `--db deploy-dev45/state.db` convention is WRONG for
this cluster**: that file is a stale June copy, the live twin DB is `~/.axrange/state.db`, and the
live surface is in `/home/rocky/axr-product/deploy-dev45/state.db`. The README's DB guidance is
rewritten to say: use a **dedicated scratch DB** for a throwaway; **sensor resolution is DB-local**,
so the sensor's surface row must be in the **same** DB; and the **dedicated-sensor path** (a scratch
manifest + `--images /home/rocky/axr-product/deploy-dev45`) is the working recipe until the standing
sensor is repaired. It also now notes that `o2 deploy` / `o2 destroy` are **classifier-blocked for
agents** and are the operator's to run.

**Why:** §9.2 is the gate that separates "the recipes render" from "a real daemon answered a real
client and the sensor named the protocol". Everything before this run was provable offline; the
three things that could not be — `ss` matching the declared set, a completed TLS handshake and a
`krb5` record in `eve.json` — are now measured. Row 5's server half stops being a claim.

**Affected docs:**
- `docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md` — §9.2 (PASSED, with the results table, the
  sensor saga and the pending operator commands), §10.2 item 10, §12
- `twin-configs/svc-verify/README.md` — the DB guidance rewrite, the dedicated-sensor recipe, the
  anonymous-SMB criterion, the 5355 note, the 82/82 op-row count, the operator-run `o2` verbs
- `CLAUDE.md` — the services blockquote (NOT run → PASSED); the max-capture-rate blockquote's
  standing-twin claim
- `docs/PRODUCT_GAPS.md` — GAP-067..GAP-078
- `docs/LESSONS.md` — LES-244..LES-250
- `docs/SESSION_STATE.md`

**Affected code:** none. (Every gap this run opened is filed, not fixed — fixing the sensor
supervision or the vnet-name adoption path inside the same window would have changed the subject
mid-measurement.)

**Links:** `docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md` §9.2; `twin-configs/svc-verify/README.md`;
`docs/design/13-golden-twin-fidelity.md` §3.3 row 5; CHANGELOG 2026-07-17 (the supervision fix that
never covered Suricata); CHANGELOG 2026-09-05 "Protocol-faithful services BUILT".

## 2026-09-05 (later) — Scorecard run 1 RAN on `dev45` (READ-ONLY, PASSED); the first golden's candidate cast installed

**Authority:** **implementation (Claude Code)** for the measurement, the cast production and its
installation; the **user gave the go-ahead for the gated run** (it is the only gate `docs/WORK_ORDER_fidelity_scorecard.md`
§7 asks for, and unlike most gated runs it stands nothing up and tears nothing down). The row set,
gate set, deployment scope and the "score the standing twin first, the fresh cast is the first
golden" sequence it measures against are **Authority: user**
(`docs/design/13-golden-twin-fidelity.md` §3.3/§3.4/§8.2).

Two things happened, one after the other: the scorecard's first subject was **measured on-cluster**,
and the **first golden's candidate subject was produced and installed**. Neither deployed, updated,
destroyed or mutated anything: run 1 is read-only by brief, and the cast's own deploy stays gated.

### A. Scorecard run 1 — measured on `dev45`, read-only, all six steps passed

Subject: the standing **`te corpapcn-twin-0e4f7d58`** (138 VMs), `completed`, plan_id
`corpapcn-twin-b6aaa3c5`. **Preflight:** the state store `~/.axrange/state.db` was pre-existing (so
GAP-060's create-on-typo trap never fired), and `deploys.ir_hash` is **byte-identical** to a
re-expansion of `twin-configs-cast/corpapcn-twin/`
(`b6aaa3c5b145cba7c24b4f7fbf4da497a8bbf2317fa72384163a7739e76cb0c0`) — the bundle on disk *is* the
one this twin was deployed from, so nothing was recorded in `subject` as a mismatch.

- **Step 1** (`--no-probe --no-write --json`, 1.2 s): **every number §7 step 1 predicted matched
  exactly** — verdict **`incomplete`**; **row 1 fail** naming `10.20.193.95`, `10.20.205.56`,
  `10.80.20.67`, `10.80.72.13`, `192.168.0.149`; **row 2 fail** — 6 of 32 observed segments, 6 of 31
  observed local cross-segment pairs, **0 unrouted**, `internet-sink` twin-only/`compiler`; **row 3
  pass** over 11 groups → 22 hosts → 27 actions (7 `provision-app` + 20 `run-script`); **row 5
  `not-computed`** over 31 facts / 10 hosts with 10 published exclusions (7 *not a service* — 7680/tcp
  Delivery Optimization and six ephemeral ≥ 49152; 2 *no protocol-aware probe* — 9997/tcp Splunk and
  3389/udp; 1 *below threshold* — `10.21.161.1:53/udp`); rows 4/6/7 **deferred**, 8/9/10 provisional
  **`not-computed`**, 11 **`not-attested`**; `provenance_sidecar: absent`; `gate_set.gating` `[1,2,3,5]`;
  `expanded_nodes: 138` / `expanded_hosts: 137` (§9.7 ruling 3's deliberate double publication,
  confirmed live).
- **Step 2** (with the probe, 111 s — the probe itself ≈ 110 s for 31 facts across 10 hosts):
  **row 5 computed, verdict `incomplete` → `not-golden`, exactly as predicted.** **5 passed / 26
  failed / 0 inconclusive / 0 unresolved / 0 unmeasured**, `probe_errors: 0`. The five passes are
  `10.20.196.1:53/udp`, `10.80.20.5:53/tcp`, `10.80.20.5:53/udp`, `10.80.64.2:53/udp` (dns) and
  `10.80.75.171:80/tcp` (http). The 26 failures: **17 timeout, 6 connection-refused, 3
  protocol-mismatch** — and the three mismatches are the most informative data in the run:
  `10.20.196.1:123/udp` ntp *"NTP mode 3, expected 4 (server)"* and `10.80.20.5:389/tcp` + `:389/udp`
  ldap/cldap *"unexpected LDAP protocolOp tag 0x63"*. Mode 3 is the **client's own request** and
  0x63 is **searchRequest**: that is the echo responder bouncing the probe's bytes back at it. An
  **open port scored zero**, correctly. Nine of the twelve methods exercised scored no passes at all,
  and **`axdc01` (`domain-controller-10-80-2`) timed out on all ten of its facts** — spec §1's *"a
  Windows domain controller that answers nothing"*, now measured rather than argued.
- **Step 3** (`--include-deferred`, 115 s): **row 4 ran** (11 hosts, 69 listen facts, per-host sets
  published) and **stayed out of the gate set** (`gating` still `[1,2,3,5]`, status `deferred`) — the
  §7 step-3 acceptance criterion. Row 5 came back **bit-identical** to step 2: the probe is
  deterministic on this twin.
- **Step 4** (API parity): the operator's long-running `axrange serve` on `:8080` is 9 days old and
  **predates the build** (404 on the route). It was **left running** — a second instance was started
  on `127.0.0.1:8010` with both config dirs, returned a document **identical to step 1's modulo
  `generated_at`**, and only that second instance was stopped.
- **Step 5** (the one write): exactly one artifact,
  `twin-configs-cast/corpapcn-twin/corpapcn-twin.scorecard.corpapcn-twin-0e4f7d58.json` — 26,519
  bytes, valid JSON, `scorecard_version: "1"`, content identical to step 1's. Left **uncommitted**
  for the orchestrator.
- **Step 6** (nothing changed): `te status` `completed`, **1242/1242 op rows completed**, 151 VMs
  total / 143 running / 138 `te-` prefixed, deploy rows still 46, `updated_at` unchanged at
  `1788204492.519899`, `git status twin-configs-cast/` showing only the new scorecard file. Guest
  **uptime monotonic across a 578 s window** on `dns-resolver-10-20-196` (439810.19 → 440388.22) and
  `dns-resolver-10-80-64` (439808.54 → 440386.59) — the W1 live-verify's "the VM was never touched"
  proof, reused. The probe's temporary `/tmp/axr-fidelity-probe.py` was **absent on all four sampled
  peer guests** afterwards: the `finally` cleanup works. **Wall: ~9.5 min** for steps 1–6, ~13 min
  with preflight.

**Five deltas from §7's expectations, recorded rather than smoothed:**

1. **`gate_active: false`, and a SECOND warning fired.** The standing twin's bundle predates the
   deployment-scope change and carries **no `observation:` block**, so it has no recorded deploy
   threshold; rows 1 and 2 were judged against spec §3.1's default 0.8 and
   `below_deploy_threshold_nodes` is **necessarily empty**. §7 predicted one warning (the sidecar
   re-derivation one, which also fired). Both warnings are in the artifact.
2. **Terminology:** rows 4/6/7 carry `status: deferred` and `verdict: not-computed` — two fields, not
   one; §7's shorthand "rows 4/6/7 `deferred`" reads as a verdict and is not.
3. **§7 step 4 was executed differently on purpose:** "restart `axrange serve` first" would have
   killed the operator's 9-day-old server, so a second instance was started beside it instead.
4. **§7's "next free id: GAP-061" pointer was stale** (061–065 were taken by the services build the
   same day). Fixed in place; next free id is **GAP-067**.
5. **The run scored the WORKING TREE, not committed code.** The mid-flight Console S1 slice had
   already reworded `axrange/fidelity/reference.py`'s sidecar-absent warning (wording only — no
   scoring logic, no row, no verdict), so the artifact's warning text is the reworded one. Said here
   so the artifact's provenance is honest.

**One expectation this run corrects, in the work order and in this file's own trail:** §10 and the
handoff both said the run would produce "the first real numbers for rows 6/8/9/10, which spec §8.3's
v1.1 tolerances are waiting on." It **did not, and could not** — rows 8/9/10 need the fingerprint
seam (**S5, unbuilt**; `reference.fingerprint: "absent"`) and a **recapture** of the deployed twin,
and row 6 is deferred by category. All four returned `not-computed`. **The v1.1 tolerance numbers
therefore wait on S5 plus a recapture, not on this run** — corrected in §7/§10 and in spec §8.3's
neighbourhood rather than left as a promise the run cannot keep.

**No GAP filed from the run.** The one near-miss — the newer `tap-corpapcn-twin-0e4f7d58-e4b517fe`
row stamped `tier: te` — is already handled by `_latest_te_deploy`'s `exclude_plan_id_prefix="tap-"`,
and that was verified on cluster rather than assumed. **One lesson, from the result rather than from
a defect: LES-229** — an open port is not a reproduced service, and only a protocol-aware probe can
tell them apart. The echo responder answered on 123/udp and 389/tcp+udp and **correctly scored
zero**, which is what makes row 5's honest 5/31 different from the flattered 8/31 an open-port check
would have reported.

### B. The first golden's candidate cast — produced, installed, verified read-only

Produced from the aximprint worktree `/home/rocky/aximprint-axrange` at commit **`a319f04`** (the
services build) against the **only** source PIT in the store, **`pit-03de65ec67-1787823960`** (11
windows, 786 entities, 32 segments, `capture_loss_pct_max` 12.35%) in
`/home/rocky/corpapcn-fidelity-artifacts/src-ir-rematerialized-20260902.duckdb` — worked from a
**scratch copy**, the store never opened directly and byte-identical afterwards. (Both aximprint work
orders name that path; some older notes carry `/home/rocky/twin-fidelity-corpapcn-2026-08-31/`, which
is not it.) Command: `cast --store … --pit pit-03de65ec67-1787823960 --imprint-id corpapcn-golden
--relocate 10.200.0.0/16`; **no assertions file present**, so the compiler took the no-assertions
path. **Two runs byte-identical** (41 files; 33 segments / 88 nodes / 31 conduits / 108 assignments /
687 provenance fields).

Installed with `scripts/install_cast_as_twin.py … twin-configs-cast corpapcn-golden --sensors
XPress-SW-A` (exit 0, no warnings — the sensor flag mirrors the standing twin's tap plan and is an
install-time **Lab** fact, not a cast one) into **`twin-configs-cast/corpapcn-golden/`**: 41 files,
1.2 MB — `corpapcn-golden.yaml` (36,799 B), `.topology.json` (732,902 B, **`bundle_schema:
axr-topology/1.4`**), `.provenance.json` (198,748 B, `axr-cast-provenance/1`, **687 fields**),
`tfc-cast.yaml` (113 assignments), `tap-cast.yaml` (32 bindings), 22 personas, 14 `serve-*.sh`.

**The sidecar LANDS.** This is the first bundle whose evidence classes the scorecard reads **from the
producer**: it loads as `provenance_sidecar: present`, `evidence_source: sidecar fields`, `warnings:
[]`. The standing twin — installed 2026-09-02, before aximprint's D9 — still loads `absent` and
re-derived, which is exactly the difference run 1's first warning names.

**axRange verification, read-only, nothing deployed.** `te validate --config corpapcn-golden
--twin-configs-dir twin-configs:twin-configs-cast --images deploy-dev45` → **PASS** with the single
`OVR-020` warning the shipped `corp-enterprise` config produces identically. The gate at
`DEPLOY_MIN_OCCUPANCY 0.8` removed **40 node specs (77 hosts)** and **9 segments** (8 gated, 1
emptied: `seg-10-5-0-0`), dropped 8 conduits, pruned 9 router legs, and emitted **48 node specs (80
hosts) on 24 segments**. `te plan` → **`corpapcn-golden-ea3a42d7`, ops=695, barriers=105,
edges=1700** — 80 `clone-vm` (64 observed + 15 internet-sink + 1 `gw`), 26 `run-script`, 23
`provision-app`, 6 `join-host-to-domain`, 4 `create-domain-user`, 1
`wait-domain-ready[axdc01-app@corp.axr.lab]`, 1 `configure-router`; uncapped (`0.0`) 157 nodes / 33
nets. 18 of 88 nodes declare `listen:` (71 ports; the 4 Windows nodes get no POSIX script); recipes
bind9 ×8, nginx ×7, chrony ×5, sshd ×4, isc-dhcp-server ×1, with 7 echo groups labelled evidence
class `compiler`. The DC `axdc01` is `windows-server-2022@v1` on `seg-10-80-20-0`, 4c/8Gi/60Gi,
`install: [{role: adds}]`, `provides: [{domain: corp.axr.lab}]`, 12 listen ports, observation 11/11
windows, occupancy 1.0, role_confidence 0.9; 4 imputed lab identities (`alice`, `bob-admin`,
`svc-sql` with a kerberoastable SPN, `jsmith` AS-REP-roastable); sole param `DEPLOY_MIN_OCCUPANCY:
0.8`. **Every figure reproduces `docs/WORK_ORDER_deploy_gate.md` §11.2 and the services CHANGELOG
entry exactly** — the gate's 80 nodes / 24 nets at 0.8 and the services build's ops=695 /
barriers=105 / edges=1700 were measured there on `corpapcn-scope-check`, and this installed bundle
reproduces both.

`twin-configs-cast/corpapcn-twin/` is **untouched** and the standing twin has **zero** new mutations.
(The two bundles' `pit_id`s differ — `pit-63994c9543-…` vs `pit-03de65ec67-…`, same `as_of`
`1787823960` — because they were materialized separately; per LES-222 `pit_id` is published and never
cross-checked, so this is expected, not a mismatch.)

**One producer↔consumer seam found and verified — GAP-066, an unconsumed seam, not a breakage.**
`axrange/fidelity/reference.py` consumes **none** of `axr-topology/1.4`'s additions: the per-listen
`services[].app` names (403 rows across 365 of 786 nodes), the top-level `observations` block (354
DNS names, 11 identity principals) and the per-listen `evidence_class`. Row 5's denominator still
reads each node's `listen_ports`, which 1.4 keeps, so **nothing breaks** — but the probe still picks
its method from a **port table** when the bundle now carries the observed **application** per listen,
and row 5's evidence mix still counts every fact `unclassified` when the producer now labels each one
`observed`. Registered as GAP-066 and cross-referenced from
`docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md` §11/§12 where the S4 seam is discussed; S4's
zones/vhosts consumer is separately gated on aximprint GAP-019.

**Still gated, and still not run:** the cast's **deploy** on `dev45` (the first golden's subject) and
the **services live-verify** on a throwaway twin (`docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md`
§9.2) — a `twin-configs/svc-verify/` bundle for the latter is **being authored by a parallel agent**
as this is written.

**Why:**

- Run 1 is the measurement the golden-twin direction rests on: a scorecard that had never touched a
  cluster is an assertion about an assertion. The probe pass is the half that could not be measured
  offline, and it is the half that produced the finding — an **open port is not a reproduced
  service**.
- The candidate cast is the first artifact on this seam that carries its own provenance, so the
  scorecard stops re-deriving evidence classes for the subject that matters. Producing and verifying
  it read-only, ahead of its gated deploy, means the deploy is the only unknown left.

**Affected docs:**
- `docs/CHANGELOG.md` — this entry
- `docs/WORK_ORDER_fidelity_scorecard.md` — header status (§7 RUN/PASSED); §7 rewritten as a result

  not predicted; the v1.1 numbers wait on S5 + a recapture; the candidate cast installed)

## 2026-09-05 — Protocol-faithful services BUILT (row 5's server half, both repos)

**Authority:** **user** for the rulings this implements — `docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md`
§10.1, ratified 2026-09-04: **D1 (a)** a real DC now (not deferred to the LDIF ingest), **D2 (b)**
give the inert `App.listen` its first consumer plus a twin-config `listen:` passthrough, **D4** the
**lab realm `corp.axr.lab`**, not the observed `AXELLIO.DOM` (the forest name is `imputed`; the DC's
observed short hostname is kept). The ruling underneath all three is
`docs/design/13-golden-twin-fidelity.md` §8.2, verbatim: *"implement realistic protocol clients and
servers based on the observed data. avoid using synthetic data generation where possible."*
**implementation (Claude Code)** for **D3 (b)** (servers stay runtime `pkg:`; bake only the client
tools), **D5 (a)** (the second DC served as Linux equivalents for the first golden, Windows
promotion as a follow-on), **D6 (a)**, **D7 (a)**, **D8 (a)** (the echo survives only where no real
server exists, labelled `compiler`, behind a render-time guard) — and for the four build calls
recorded below: the **conditional** `node_listen` carrier, `listen: []` refused at the author, the
**narrowed** multi-bound-app refusal, and the QUIC exclusion in the scorecard's denominator.

**This is the SERVER half of row 5.** The client half (`drive: command`,
`docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_traffic.md`, BUILT 2026-09-02) is unchanged; the two meet on the
wire, which is the point — a real `dig` needs a real resolver on the other end, and until now the
other end echoed the query back.

**What changed — axRange (this repo).**

- **S1, the declared listen set.** `App.listen` — present in `axrange/ir/schema.py` since v1,
  documented as *"informational for network policy generation"*, with **zero readers anywhere and
  zero shipped content setting it** — **gained its first consumer**. A twin config's
  `nodes[].listen: [{port, proto}]` (new `TwinConfigListen` + `TwinConfigNode.listen`) passes
  through `twinconfig.expand` to the node's app; `clone-vm` carries it as a `node_listen` resolved
  input; `planner.inventory.build_twin_inventory` publishes it as `hosts[].listen`
  (`HostListen`/`HostInfo.listen`); `axrange te inventory` prints `listen=53/tcp,53/udp,…`;
  `GET /api/deploys/{id}/inventory` carries `HostListenDTO`; the Grafana plugin's `api.ts` gains the
  matching type (no UI reads it yet — the shape is published so a consumer binds to it rather than
  re-deriving it). **App synthesis now fires on `install` OR `listen`**, since `App` is the only IR
  object with a `listen` field and a declare-only host still needs somewhere to put it; an app with
  no `install:` emits **no op**, so the DAG is unchanged. `_declared_listen_by_host` does the
  `apps[].listen` → `bindings[]` join once, in `pass1_emit`.
- **Three build calls inside S1, each with a stated cost.** (1) **`clone-vm` carries `node_listen`
  only when non-empty.** Unconditional emission would put a `None` on the NEW side of
  `plandiff._diff_fields` for every host of every standing twin, whose persisted rows have no such
  key — ABSENT vs None is a difference, so **every** `clone-vm` would classify Mutated and
  `te deploy --update` would hard-refuse on 100% of already-deployed content (LES-185's shape, one
  build later). **Accepted cost, pinned by a test:** *changing* a declared set on a standing twin
  is a named `--update` refusal, so the declared set is a deploy-time fact, not a runtime-swappable
  one like `role` (which is exactly why `OP-TFC-020 assign-role` exists). (2) **`listen: []` is
  refused at the author** (`expand._check_listen`): every layer downstream reads ABSENT as "this
  twin makes no claim about this host", and there is no second meaning for "claims to serve
  nothing", so an empty list silently collapses into the first — the `tap_points: []` precedent.
  (3) **`_emit_apps` refuses a multi-bound app only when it declares `listen:`** — narrowed on
  purpose from a first cut that refused all of them and was caught immediately by shipped content
  (see GAP-065).
- **The scorecard reports the declared set, and it can never move a verdict.**
  `Reproduced.declared_listen` + `declared_vs_observed_listen()` publish declared-vs-observed counts
  and deltas in rows 4 and 5's `measured` block only — never a clause, never a gate. A declaration
  is not evidence: row 4 (in-guest `ss -lnH -tu`) and row 5 (a protocol-aware probe) stay the
  measurement of record, which is precisely the substitution this work order exists to remove. **All
  four (declared × observed) present/absent cells report an absence as an absence, never as a
  mismatch**, and every branch publishes the same key set so the document stays diffable; a
  strip-and-compare test pins that stripping the declared keys leaves every verdict identical.
- **The QUIC exclusion.** `(443, "udp")` joins `axrange/fidelity/probe.py`'s `_EXCLUSIONS` as *no
  protocol-aware probe*, and row 5's published `denominator_note` is now **derived from that table**
  rather than restating it — the first hand-written copy had already gone stale in the same review
  round that added QUIC.
- **S3 (the axRange half), the `ad-cast` fixture** (`tests/fixtures/twin-configs/ad-cast/` +
  `scripts/enable-rdp.ps1`): a **cast-shaped** twin config — `observation:` blocks, a `count:` group,
  declared listen sets — proving the twin-config path end to end, because the shipped precedent
  (`twin-configs/corp-enterprise/`) is hand-authored and S3's acceptance is specifically the cast's
  path. `axdc01` carries `install: [{role: adds}]` + `provides: [{domain: corp.axr.lab}]` and
  declares **ten service ports** (the observed twelve minus the two dynamic RPC allocations, which
  are not services — GAP-064); `count: 2` members carry `joins:` and an RDP-enabling `run-script`
  (bounded 60 s listener poll, firewall group addressed by its locale-independent resource id).
  Proven in the suite: the twin-config path emits `provision-app` + `wait-domain-ready` + the
  `domain-ready` barrier, joins order after it, the RDP script after `host-ready`,
  `render_join_to_domain` bakes the DC's allocated IP as a literal, and `role: adds` without
  `provides:` still refuses loudly. Lab-default identity users are marked imputed **in prose** — the
  twin-config vocabulary has no per-fact evidence marker; the provenance sidecar is the
  machine-readable home (`docs/design/14-operator-assertions.md` §2).
- **S5, the honest-gap register:** GAP-061 (QUIC 443/udp), GAP-062 (proprietary 9997/tcp, and
  3389/udp for a different reason), GAP-063 (8006 served as real HTTPS, which is protocol-valid and
  application-unfaithful), GAP-064 (ephemeral/dynamic ports + Windows Delivery Optimization 7680) —
  each cross-referenced from `probe.classify_listen_port`, so adding an exclusion row and forgetting
  the register is visible from the code.

**What changed — aximprint** (worktree `/home/rocky/aximprint-axrange`, `wip/golden-twin`, built in
parallel, **uncommitted**; its own CHANGELOG 2026-09-05 carries the full detail). New
`synth/services.py`: the topology export bumps to **`bundle_schema: axr-topology/1.4`** with per-node
**`services[]`** (`{port, proto, evidence_class: observed, app?, app_evidence_class?}`, the app token
spelled in the **sensor's** vocabulary so it joins against `edges[].proto`) and a new top-level
**`observations`** block limited to what the belief store actually holds — `dns.names` and
`identity.principals`, and **no** `realm`/`spns`/`tls`/`http`/`smb` keys, because nothing was
fabricated to fill the sketch. The cast emits `listen: [{port, proto}]` per node **from the observed
listen census, never the persona dial set** — the input correction that is half the point of the
slice. Fourteen `serve-*.sh` real-daemon recipes replace the echo (bind9 on 8 node groups, nginx on
7, chrony on 5, an sshd assertion on 4, isc-dhcp-server on 1), **one script per node GROUP, not per
role** (see LES-227), each fail-loud via `systemd-run` + `systemctl is-active` after a config check;
the echo survives only on stated-gap ports, labelled `compiler`, behind a render-time contention
guard (D8). The sink's real services are derived from a census of the **external** listener
population it stands in for. The `domain-controller` node is authored with `role: adds` +
`provides: corp.axr.lab` + `joins:` on the members + an imputed `identity:` block; the `roles.yaml`
revert is reversed and the fixed-point checker's two DC exceptions are **deleted** rather than
re-worded (24 catalog rows, 24 fixed points).

**Measured on the CorpAPCN cast:** 88 node groups, **18 declaring a `listen:` set** (4 of them
Windows, which get no POSIX recipe); 14 `serve-*.sh` emitted; 7 groups still carrying a labelled
echo, every remaining port a **stated gap** rather than an oversight (135/tcp ×3, 389/tcp+udp ×2,
445/tcp ×1 — the second DC's Linux equivalents, D5(a); 443/udp ×3 — QUIC; 9997/tcp ×1 — Splunk;
137/138/49667/55390/57086/63153 — not services). Two runs byte-identical. The installed cast passes
**axRange's own validator** (`Result: PASS`, one `OVR-020` warning the shipped `corp-enterprise`
config produces identically) and **plans: ops=695, barriers=105, edges=1700**, including
`wait-domain-ready[axdc01-app@corp.axr.lab]`, 4 `create-domain-user` and 6 `join-host-to-domain`.

**Two corrections to this work order's own §6.1 seam sketch, reported rather than papered over.**
(1) **There is no per-listen `windows_seen`** — `ir_entity.listen_ports` is a bare
`["<port>/<proto>"]` array and the entity's `windows_seen` is a *host's* occupancy, not a *port's*;
publishing it under that name would attach a plausible number to a fact nothing measured. The
consequence lands here: **row 4's per-listen occupancy has no producer**, and the in-guest `ss`
check stays its only source. (2) **DNS rows are `evidence_class: referenced`, not `observed`** — the
capture saw a *name in an answer*, not the host — and the sketch's `server`/`qtype`/`count` fields do
not exist at the IR boundary at all. §6.1 is amended in place with the original struck through.

**`App.listen` gained its first consumer with NO version bump.** The field, its shape and its
optionality are unchanged, and no shipped content set it before this build, so every pre-existing IR
expands and hashes byte-identically — the permanent `plan_id`/`ir_hash` pin over all five shipped
`twin-configs/` still holds (LES-126). **Shape enforcement lives at the twin-config layer**
(`expand._check_listen`), **not in the validator**: no catalog rule references `App.listen`, so
`VALIDATOR` stays **114** rules and `OP_CATALOG` stays **40** op types. No
`IR_SCHEMA`/`PLANNER`/`ADAPTER`/`IMAGE_MANIFEST` change, no `scenario_schema_version` bump, no
state-store bump, no new op type. **Full suite: 2318 passed / 12 skipped / 0 failed, 2330
collected** (44 new cases in `tests/test_protocol_faithful_services.py`, plus extensions to the
fidelity and twin-config catalog suites).

**Gaps filed:** GAP-061..064 (S5's register, above) and **GAP-065** — an app bound to two hosts is
provisioned on only the **first**, silently, and three shipped `target-environments/enterprise-multisite`
apps do exactly that (`db-engine`, `etl-runner`, `branch-file`, each with an author comment saying
the two-host binding is deliberate). Pre-existing, found by shipped content when a first-cut refusal
tripped on it (LES-185's shape again). Only the case this build makes *wrong* rather than merely
incomplete is fenced — a multi-bound app that declares `listen:` would publish ports for a host that
installs nothing.

**Live-verify:** the mandatory gated `dev45` run (§9.2 — a purpose-built throwaway twin: router,
bind9, nginx, chrony, a `windows-server-2022` DC, a joined `win11-finance` member, a
`drive: command` client, a tap plan to a standing `suricata-sensor`) has **NOT** run. Both halves
are unit/suite-verified and plan-verified only. **Never against the standing
`te corpapcn-twin-0e4f7d58`** — it is do-not-destroy, it is scorecard run 1's subject, and a new
declared listen set on it would trip `--update`'s mutation refusal by this build's own design.

**Decisions queued for the user (neither taken here):** **GAP-065** — fan the multi-bound app out
over every binding, or refuse and split the three shipped apps into one entry per host; both change
a shipped scenario's op set or its `ir_hash`/`plan_id`, so neither is a drive-by. **aximprint
GAP-019** — a carrier for the observed TLS server names / HTTP hosts / SMB shares, which the belief
store already holds (8 distinct `serves_ssl` names; `serves_http` binds `test-api.swlab.axellio.dom`
to the twin's own `10.80.75.171`) but which `ir/materialize.py` never crosses into a PIT; adding one
is an **additive IR minor bump** on aximprint's one ratified schema, and it gates **S4**.

**What is NOT built:** S4 (observed protocol content — zones from observed names, vhosts from
observed SNI/`Host`/URI) is gated on that carrier; D5(b) (the second DC promoted as a real Windows
DC) is a follow-on; Windows nodes get no service recipe at all (aximprint GAP-018) beyond the DC's
promotion and the members' RDP script.

**Affected docs:**
- `docs/CHANGELOG.md` — this entry
- `docs/WORK_ORDER_protocol_faithful_services.md` — status → BUILT; new §10.2 "Built as"; §6.1
  amended for the two corrections and the real seam shape (original struck through); §8 per-slice

**Affected code:**

- `axrange/planner/pass1_emit.py` (`_declared_listen_by_host`, the conditional `node_listen`, the
  narrowed multi-bound refusal), `axrange/planner/inventory.py` (`HostListen`, `HostInfo.listen`)
- `axrange/cli/main.py` (`te inventory` `listen=`), `axrange/api/{dtos,service}.py` (`HostListenDTO`),
